done-criterion: ONE VERSIONED ACT SHIPPING THREE HALVES TOGETHER, because the pin contradicts the one-schema-per-repo law as written (T-a2): the law text in CLAUDE.md gains the record-kind exemption, check_schema_agreement gains its EXECUTABLE branch (closed-arc homes checked as PINNED-at-close, never against the floor), and the migration participates - law text, carrier contract and checker moving as one, never prose ahead of mechanism. carrier_homes reverses its glob exclusion for the LIVE arc home ONLY, and the reversal NAMES what the exclusion protected (N9: it kept closure records out of the rewrite path, which becomes explicit in the closed kind's exemption instead of implicit in a parse-time guard). plugin/hooks/pre-commit is IN this act (astra-a6): it hardcodes the three carrier kinds and rejects glob homes, and the staged arc-shape check is part of this act's red-first, going red on a hand-mangled arc file. DRY RUN FIRST, over the population named BY COMMAND at run time - find ~/dev -path '*/.claude/lifecycle.json' - never a restated count, with the -name spelling recorded as the arrangement's NEGATIVE CONTROL because bfs never matches a slashed pattern with it. THE APPLY IS NOT THIS DESK'S: this entry ENDS at the dry-run evidence, which halts to the round desk with its per-repo output, the lc-218 pattern. Read-back binds when the apply runs (law 25): it asserts EVERY PLANNED CHANGE arrived per target, never the schema number, which was already correct before the write and is the I9b defect by name. MUST-NOT-BUILD: no apply from here; no reversal for the CLOSED arc home; nothing that makes a schema bump rewrite the archive.
evidence: MEASURED at this desk 2026-09-19, the population by the design's own command with its own control: find ~/dev -path '*/.claude/lifecycle.json' returns 19 files; the -name spelling returns 0, so the dead form is confirmed dead on this machine rather than assumed, and the control discriminates instead of merely agreeing. MEASURED, the split inside that 19: 9 sit under ~/dev/local/statiker-run-* and are throwaway run arms and clones, leaving 10 real repos - which is the figure LEDGER.md:106 already carries as TEN repos carry a declaration, so two independently-taken readings agree on the same coordinate. MEASURED: ~/.config/lifecycle/repos lists ONE repo (~/dev/Gunther-Schulz/dotfiles) and does not list lifecycle itself, which is the router-roster finding booked separately. DERIVED, read at the artifact: carrier_homes lives at declaration.py:1878-1907 hardcoded to items, done and ledger and dropping glob homes; check_schema_agreement at declaration.py:1974-2002 has no record exemption; plugin/hooks/pre-commit hardcodes the three carriers and rejects glob homes. RELAYED from the round desk, carried as theirs: the closed-body version pin is the round's one r2 design judgment (N9), made against attack pressure rather than inherited.

amend-reason: 2026-09-19 tools/prove-rows.py JOINS the write-set, and this is the SECOND item in this wave to need it — lc-241 needed it for the same reason. The class is now visible rather than incidental: ANY change to the source LINE an emit site sits on breaks that row's recorded mutation anchor, because the anchor is the literal line. Here the reach change re-indented one-schema-per-repo's own comparison into a per-body loop, and schema_mismatch's anchor stopped matching. It is law 24's must-move-with half, never its realizes half: the checker works without the file, and the file depends LOUDLY. Booked on the entry rather than left for the next reader to rediscover as the same red — and worth the round's notice as a booking-time rule, since a write-set that omits it hands the join a confident non-collision every time a refusal's site moves.

Shipping the watch here would either split one hook change across two items or answer COULD NOT VERIFY on every future arc commit ahead of its own consumer. closed-reason: 2026-09-19 CLOSED ON THE DRY-RUN EVIDENCE, which is where this entry's criterion ends by construction - the APPLY was never this desk's. THE IN-REPO HALF, one versioned act: the law text gained the record-kind exemption stated as a kind of CARRIER rather than a file list; check_schema_agreement gained its executable branch and reaches every body a glob home names; the migration plan follows the same expansion, naming every file it would rewrite rather than printing one line for a change landing in N. carrier_homes reverses the glob exclusion for the LIVE arc home ONLY, and the closed home's ABSENCE from that list IS the pin - what the exclusion protected is now explicit instead of implicit in a parse-time guard. THE DRY RUN, population re-derived BY COMMAND at run time and never restated: 19 declaration files under the dev root; reached-set DELTA in ZERO of them, because the reversal is keyed to the live arc home and no repo declares one yet. Read-only was MEASURED, not asserted - each repo's git status fingerprinted before and after, all 19 unchanged - and the arrangement's negative control still discriminates: the -name spelling returns 0 where -path returns 19. THE APPLY DISSOLVED ON THAT MEASUREMENT, ruled at the round desk: an apply over a zero-delta population is an act with no object, so what survives is its live moment - the reversal becomes real per repo exactly when an arc kind lands in that repo's declaration, which is lc-231 here, where kind check and the suite are the apply's own instruments. Any future apply derives its population at the act. N7's cross-repo sequencing is SATISFIED: the reach act is on the remote before lc-239 beat 1 opens anything. SUPERSEDED IN SCOPE, recorded rather than silently dropped: plugin/hooks/pre-commit left this write-set by ledger decision 2026-09-19 - the hook half moves WHOLE to lc-231 with the arc-shape checker it routes to and with its staged-arc-shape red-first, so astra-a6 is discharged there rather than here. RED-FIRST, with its honest limit: four arms - live glob home REACHED, closed home EXEMPT, matching live body clean, and a repo declaring no arcs reaching exactly the three homes it always did (the MUST-NOT-MOVE arm) - of which TWO passed trivially before the fix, when nothing was reached at all, and only became discriminating after it. MUST-NOT-BUILD held: no apply from here, no reversal for the CLOSED arc home, and nothing that makes a bump rewrite the archive. GREEN at 3275177: suite 865 OK; --test 117 rows CLEAN; kind check CLEAN over 26 kinds; item check and conservation CLEAN. The lc-30 dependent fired and was repaired rather than waived - schema_mismatch's recorded anchor is the literal comparison line this act re-indented, re-anchored with tools/prove-rows.py added to the write-set, the second instance of that class in this wave. CARRIED OUT, for the round's record and not this item's: 17 of the 19 declared repos sit at schema 2 against this build's 6.

## lc-231
grade: DONE
requirement: AN ARC IS THE ONE THING THIS SYSTEM IS ABOUT AND THE ONLY THING IT DOES NOT REGISTER. Every persisted thing resolves to a registered kind (invariant 1), and 26 kinds are declared; the ARC -- the multi-session unit of work that statiker and daneel maintain and that dies with its protocol run -- is not among them. The design of record names this exactly: those protocols capture in-flight and richly, and what they do NOT do is outlive their own arc, so session 11 re-derives what session 3 knew. Lifecycle's job is to be what they write into. Record: docs/directives/2026-09-19-arc-walk-synthesis.md, and docs/answerable-not-felt.md section 'What statiker and daneel already do'.
goal: enforce-the-invariants
write-set: UNKNOWN
done-criterion: The arc's schema is DECIDED and declared, and the decision is graded against the eight synthesis requirements rather than against walk 1 alone. The eight, which the design round answers one by one: stage exits take the full trigger vocabulary (predicate, verb, AND operator-judged); stages carry conduct constraints; the narrowing's SHAPE is a per-arc slot (eliminative / palette-with-dispositions / none); a verdict-capture seam books operator taste judgments at utterance; arcs are OPTIONAL instances, never a per-project obligation; arc-vs-lane unification is checked FIRST; external-event exits get intake observers and deadlines get a dated slot with an observer, exempt by design from the time-word ban; and outward stages are MARKED, wiring the carve-out floor into structure. THE GRADING BAR, and it is why the walks are not decoration: requirements 4, 6 and 7 are ones walk 1 alone would have got WRONG rather than merely missed, so a design justified only by the diagnosis-shaped walk fails this criterion even if it is internally consistent -- it would hardcode eliminative narrowing, predicate-only exits, and no verdict capture. MUST-NOT-BUILD: no schema change lands before lc-218's bump, per lc-218's own MUST-NOT-BUILD; an eighth or later stage is its own numbered bump.
evidence: THE DESIGN CORPUS IS TWO ARTIFACTS AND THIS ENTRY'S MESSAGE HISTORY IS NEITHER, stated because addendum 3 ruled exactly that: the doc is the carrier, the channel is not. (1) docs/directives/2026-09-19-arc-walk-synthesis.md -- four hypothetical walks over real specimens (convergent diagnosis, the Marvel Rivals freeze arc; divergent/creative, the wan2gp workflow search; routine maintenance, CachyOS general maintenance; external-consequence procedure, the B-Plan planning work) yielding eight schema requirements no single walk shows. (2) Its section 'Additional inputs': the live-working-state framing with its KILL TEST, and the belief vocabulary -- facts versus beliefs, bases, kill-conditions, citers, and reopen-WITH-PROPAGATION. The file's own closing line binds them: this file and those entry inputs are one design corpus and the design round reads both. DERIVED, not measured: the four walks are hypothetical by construction, so the eight requirements are a reasoned enumeration and not a measurement, and the entry rests on them as design inputs rather than as findings. MEASURED at this desk 2026-09-19: no 'arc' and no 'lane' kind exists in the declaration's 26, and no ledger entry rules on unification -- which is what makes requirement 6 a live first question rather than a formality. NOTE FOR THE DESIGN ROUND, found while booking this: the synthesis file's section heading says these inputs are 'already on the arc-kind entry', and no arc-kind entry existed until this one. The claim was true of a channel, not of a carrier.

I want to find the root cause; the LLM is smart enough but the environment doesn't facilitate walking the path.' SPECIMEN it was stated over, MEASURED at the dd session and relayed: an instrument's field believed unvalidated through repeated rounds, the repo's own instrument-validation runbook never entered because nothing fired its read, the flat round series visible only to the operator, and root-cause progress arriving only under repeated operator pressure. DESIGN CONSEQUENCE, recorded as INPUT and not decision: the arc kind is the JOURNEY owner, holding the path's steps, which step the arc is at, and what each step OWES (an instrument validation, a series review), so that path state becomes queryable the way lc-224's reader moments made read moments queryable; protocol skills carry the path WITHIN one run, and the arc kind is what makes it survive across sessions. SECOND HALF, same source and same grade: THE A-TO-Z INCLUDES WRITING THE MAP. The journey's close authors the environment piece the next traveler needs (a runbook, a check, a trigger) WITHOUT the operator triggering it; today every such artifact on this machine is congealed operator frustration, the design doc's own earned-by-attrition line, and the loop map grades the incident-to-rule arrow observer:MEMORY. DERIVED inputs: (1) the trigger moments become observable events once arcs have identity -- an arc closing with no runbook cited, a second occurrence of a journey shape, a probe used twice, an operator question asked twice (M5's mine); the second-occurrence one is countable ONLY once arcs are a kind, which is this item's own justification. (2) the enforcement form is the required-slot pattern at arc close, runbook: cited <name> / minted <name> / declined <reason> -- presence computable, fill judgment, a silent skip impossible, declines auditable. (3) the boundary, stated so the round does not over-design: the environment fires the QUESTION and makes writing the lazy path, while whether a piece is WORTH minting stays judgment, operator as taste and never as trigger. Cross-reference M5's drain and the close ceremony (lc-97 territory) rather than duplicating either.

amend-reason: 2026-09-19 the criterion moves from the DESIGN bar to the BUILD bar, ruled at the round desk and executed here. The design bar (schema decided and declared, graded against the eight requirements) was written when no design existed; the design now exists, locked four times and attacked twice by fresh context. Read strictly, DECLARED means the kinds sit in the live declaration, which no design document can satisfy - so the entry cannot close as a design-only item, and its requirement text is already the kinds-and-verbs requirement, which is why a sibling booking would be the duplicate home invariant 3 forbids. The design half is recorded MET rather than deleted, so what was achieved is not lost by the rewrite.
amended-done-criterion: 2026-09-19 THE DESIGN HALF IS MET AND RECORDED AS MET: the arc's schema is decided and graded against the eight synthesis requirements at the v4 fourth lock (2c8c37b), and the two kind declaration objects were VALIDATED BY EXECUTION through _validate_kind against a ref_world of the live declaration plus both kinds - zero shape findings, only the self-clearing dangling references to the not-yet-built arc verb family, with items and done bodies as negative controls returning zero (docs/2026-09-19-arc-kind-design.md, the validated-by-execution paragraph). WHAT REMAINS, and it is now this entry's criterion, is the BUILD. Done when: the two kinds - arcs and closed arcs - sit in the LIVE declaration and pass kind check with no undeclared stage; the verb family ships (open, advance, narrow, verdict, premise, belief, reopen, deadline, yield, status, close) with the design's refusal seams, namely arc advance and arc close REFUSING while undispositioned re-derive flags exist, and arc deadline REGISTERING each generated lane in the declaration's lanes list while the three retirement verbs DEREGISTER it; arcs/INDEX.md carries the head counters written by arc open and arc close in the SAME ACT as the file write (law 9), with conservation reading opened minus closed equals live files and closed equals closed files, signs per this repo's convention - fewer bodies than admissions is SHORT and the loss side, more is OVER and recoverable; the growth alarm stays SEPARATE from conservation and its EXIT_VERBS map becomes keyed on (kind, action) rather than on action alone, so adding arc close to the move tuple cannot silence the items growth alarm; and the arc block gets its OWN renderer in arcs.py following items.py's idiom, never a reuse of render_block, which renders ITEM shape. RED-FIRST SET, each green only after it has first gone red: conservation plants using INDEX counters with the corrected signs; the consuming-seam refusal pair (a stale flag blocks advance, a dispositioned flag passes); the generated deadline lane passing kind check and a retired one leaving neither body nor row, including an abandoned arc leaving zero lanes; and the pre-commit arc-shape check going red on a hand-mangled arc file. Build conduct binds per row: admitted on a PAIR (lc-142), neighbouring arrangement re-checked (lc-30). NOT THIS ENTRY'S: the carrier_homes REACH act is its own law-25 item; the KILL TEST and the ONE PILOT ARC belong to lc-239 beat 1, not here. MUST-NOT-BUILD, and its precondition is now SATISFIED: no schema change lands before lc-218's bump - lc-218 is DONE at ITEMS-DONE.md:1402, verified independently at the round desk and at this one. Also unchanged: an eighth or later stage would be its own numbered bump, and this entry adds none.

closed-reason: 2026-09-19 2026-09-19 CLOSED CLAUSE BY CLAUSE against the amended criterion re-read at the carrier, each clause named with the instrument that answered it. (1) THE KINDS ARE IN THE LIVE DECLARATION: `kind check` CLEAN at 29 kinds, every stage declared, declaration visible to git. DEVIATION, ruled at the round desk and now landed in the design document itself (b72da79) rather than only in the ledger: the criterion says TWO kinds and the build ships THREE. The index sits inside the `arcs` home glob, so by invariant 1 it resolves to the wrong kind, and four readers were carrying name-based exclusions to work around it. MEASURED, and it is what forced the extensionless `arcs/INDEX`: pathlib's glob MATCHES a leading-dot file where a shell glob does not, so `arcs/.index.md` does NOT escape `arcs/*.md` here and only dropping the `.md` does. Its growth stage is the FIRST LIVE USE of the OOV arm (D-3), reached by contact rather than by a sweep. (2) THE VERB FAMILY SHIPS: the eleven the criterion names, plus `arc disposition`, which is the consuming seam's answering verb and without which the refusal in the next clause would be a dead end. The seams: `arc advance` and `arc close` both refuse while an undispositioned re-derive flag stands (test_advance_REFUSES_while_a_belief_flag_stands / test_advance_WORKS_once_dispositioned; test_a_disposition_clears_the_flag_and_close_then_works; and test_LAST_ACT_WINS_so_a_re_reopen_blocks_again, because a flag cleared once must not stay cleared). `arc deadline` REGISTERS its generated lane in the declaration's lanes list and retirement DEREGISTERS it, body and row together, row first so a crash between them is a `kind check` finding that names itself rather than an invisible orphan. READING STATED: the design's THREE retirement verbs are realised as TWO entry points - `arc advance`, `arc close`, and `arc close --abandon` - because abandon is the same act with a different reason and the design's own verb list never carried it as a sibling; both paths call the one retirement helper (test_ADVANCING_past_the_stage_retires_body_AND_row, test_a_LATER_stages_deadline_is_NOT_retired_by_an_advance as its discriminating control, test_ABANDONING_the_arc_leaves_ZERO_lanes). (3) THE COUNTER IS WRITTEN IN THE SAME ACT AS THE BODY (law 9), proven by test_the_open_COMMITS_body_and_counter_together, and the ORDER puts the crash window on the recoverable side by construction: body first, so a crash before the counter reads OVER (the homes hold more than was admitted) and never SHORT. Conservation reads opened minus closed equals live files AND closed equals closed files, with this repo's signs - fewer bodies than admissions is SHORT and the loss side, more is OVER and recoverable - each sign planted separately (test_a_body_gone_by_a_path_that_is_not_a_closure_is_SHORT, test_an_interrupted_close_is_OVER_and_says_it_is_recoverable, test_the_closed_counter_and_the_closed_home_are_compared), and an ABSENT index answers COULD NOT VERIFY rather than zero (test_an_ABSENT_index_is_not_zero). (4) THE GROWTH ALARM STAYS SEPARATE AND ITS MAP IS KIND-AWARE - and this clause was NOT taken from the code, which is where it would have been easy to take it: EXIT_VERBS is keyed on (kind, action) and cli.py composes the right verb string, so it LOOKED met. MEASURED instead in an isolated scratch repo (XDG_STATE_HOME redirected, so the machine-wide fire log was never written) as a DISCRIMINATING PAIR: two arcs open and no close gives `exit events: 0 (arc close)` and FINDING [kind_grew_without_exit]; one `arc close` fired gives `exit events: 1 (arc close)` and growth check CLEAN. The log carries the verb spelled `arc close`, repo-scoped. And the collision EXIT_VERBS' own comment warns about does NOT occur: in that same audit run the `items` kind still reads `exit events: 0 (item close)` with an `arc close` sitting in the log. (5) THE ARC BLOCK HAS ITS OWN RENDERER: `arcs.render_arc` in arcs.py; items.py's `render_block` renders ITEM shape and is not called from the arc path. (6) RED-FIRST SET, all four arms present and each green only after its own red - the conservation plants above; the consuming-seam pair; the generated lane passing `kind check` (test_the_generated_lane_PASSES_kind_check) with retirement leaving neither body nor row and an abandoned arc leaving zero; and the pre-commit arc-shape check going red on a hand-mangled body (test_a_hand_mangled_arc_body_STOPS_the_commit, with test_a_verb_written_arc_body_passes as its control). (7) BUILD CONDUCT. lc-142: every one of the five new rows admitted on a PAIR, and `lifecycle --test` runs all 122 plant-and-control arms green. lc-30: the neighbour re-check RUN rather than assumed - prove-rows over the whole roster, 94 PROVEN, and the only two non-green rows are PRE-EXISTING and already booked, verified against this desk's own 4f0bca8 baseline before any work (amend_nothing_to_amend FAILED, lc-106's class with its specimen already on that entry; capture_dominated COULD NOT VERIFY, lc-197). No arc row darkened a neighbour. FOUND BY THAT RUN AND REPAIRED INSIDE THIS ITEM (acdf00e): the five arc rows had carried their admission pairs and NO prove-rows arrangement. The gap was exact and countable - 96 mutations of 117 rows before the arc rows landed, 96 of 122 after, so the delta was precisely this family. The pair and the arrangement are DIFFERENT CLAIMS: the pair proves the row fires on its plant and stays quiet on its control, the arrangement proves the verdict comes from the condition the code names, and this repo carries the live specimen of a row holding the first while failing the second. All five now PROVEN, each darkening exactly its own row; 101 of 122 carry an arrangement. ## lc-244
grade: DONE
requirement: EVIDENCE ROTS BETWEEN BOOKING AND PICKUP AND NOTHING SAYS WHICH EVIDENCE WAS PERISHABLE, so a claim measured once is read forever as though it still held - the arc's own measured class, evidence amended in 39 percent of completed items. A mark that carries its own date and its own re-derivation command makes staleness answerable instead of felt. Record: docs/2026-09-19-wave-designs.md section W2; decision D-5 at LEDGER.md:113 and its E10 form correction at LEDGER.md:121.
goal: enforce-the-invariants
write-set: plugin/cli/lifecycle_core/items.py,plugin/cli/lifecycle_core/cli.py,plugin/cli/lifecycle_core/refusals.py,plugin/cli/lifecycle_core/vocab.py,test/test_items.py
done-criterion: THE MARK CARRIES ITS OWN DATE: PERISHABLE(<date>, re-derive: <command>), which is what meets law 26 at the base-slot case - the first mark anyone writes has no comparison input, and a form without its own date has no computable absence there (B7). FRESHNESS SEMANTICS, stated so the flag is falsifiable: 'PERISHABLE, never re-derived' fires if and only if no evidence amendment STRICTLY LATER than the mark's date carries a re-derive result naming this mark's command; SAME-DAY RESOLVES BY BLOCK POSITION, not by date arithmetic - an amendment SEQUENCED AFTER the mark's introducing line counts as later, same-day included, because amendment order in a block is the carrier's own total order and dates alone would false-flag an immediate legitimate re-derivation, which is a guard firing on legitimate work (T-c3, law 11). A claim with no amendments and a past-dated mark FIRES. THE RE-DERIVATION ACT IS A WRITE, not a memory: item amend --evidence appends the dated result, which is the answer to W-8 (a demand that writes nothing at the consuming moment is a demand measured at zero). A REFUSAL ROW for the mark grammar (W-9), red-first on the malformed-PERISHABLE-beside-a-valid-MEASURED sibling, because the existing predicate is presence-only and a mixed slot passes it. NO-YIELD DISPOSITION, OWNED: the same retirement pass, same writer, same event as W1's. MUST-NOT-BUILD: nothing that grades the re-derivation's RESULT, and no freshness rule that reads the clock rather than the carrier's order.
evidence: DERIVED from the attack corpora, both arms independently: PERISHABLE as first designed proved a warning was RENDERED, never that evidence was refreshed (astra, NOTABLE), and the parser tests missed the sibling where a valid MEASURED token beside a malformed PERISHABLE passes the any-mark-present predicate - an entirely unmarked comparison is not a control for that case. MEASURED, read at the artifact 2026-09-19: EVIDENCE_MARKS at items.py:293 is the four-member closed set MEASURED, DERIVED, RECALLED, RELAYED, and evidence_mark_problem is documented presence-only at items.py:286-292, so the grammar check this entry adds is a NEW refusal and refusals.py is in the write-set for it. RELAYED from the round desk, carried as theirs: the evidence-MARK member form STANDS over D-5's original slot-marker wording, recorded as a supersession rather than left as the silent re-forming W-7 caught - marks are what the admission door already checks, so presence enforces free. DERIVED: the same-day block-position rule exists because terra's over-constraint lens found the date-only form deeming a normal immediate repair stale until tomorrow.

closed-reason: 2026-09-19 2026-09-19 CLOSED CLAUSE BY CLAUSE against the criterion re-read at the carrier. THE MARK CARRIES ITS OWN DATE: `PERISHABLE(<date>, re-derive: <command>)` ships as a FIFTH EVIDENCE MARK rather than a slot marker, which is the round's E10 form correction over D-5 and is what makes presence enforce for free - it joins the set the admission door already checks, where a marker in a new place would have needed a new enforcement path and been enforced nowhere until somebody built one. Both arguments are load-bearing and the criterion names why: the DATE meets law 26 at the base-slot case, since the first mark anyone writes has no comparison input and a form without its own date has no computable absence at exactly that case (B7); the COMMAND is what makes staleness answerable, because a flag saying a claim may be stale and stopping there hands its reader the work of rediscovering how the claim was taken. FRESHNESS SEMANTICS, STATED AND FALSIFIABLE: the flag fires iff no evidence write SEQUENCED AFTER the mark's introducing line names that mark's command. SAME-DAY RESOLVES BY BLOCK POSITION and never by date arithmetic - amendments are appended, so the carrier's own order IS the total order over evidence writes, and a date-only rule would deem a NORMAL IMMEDIATE REPAIR stale until tomorrow, flagging an author for doing exactly the right thing (T-c3, law 11). THE CLOCK APPEARS ONCE AND ONLY WHERE SEQUENCE CANNOT ANSWER - has the mark's day passed - which is the criterion's own past-dated case; a mark written today has not yet had the day in which its re-derivation could arrive, so firing on it would fire before the opportunity existed. READING STATED so it can be reversed: the criterion's `if and only if` read alone would fire on a mark written minutes ago, and its own worked sentence says PAST-dated FIRES, so the day-passed guard is the reading that makes both sentences true at once. THE RE-DERIVATION IS A WRITE (W-8): `item amend --evidence` appends the dated result and that append is what clears the flag - a demand that writes nothing at the consuming moment is a demand measured at zero. THE REFUSAL ROW FOR THE MARK GRAMMAR (W-9) is registered and red-first on the case the attack found: its plant carries a VALID `MEASURED` sibling BESIDE a malformed PERISHABLE, because the existing mark predicate is presence-only and a valid sibling satisfies it outright - so the malformed mark would ship, the freshness read would never match it, and the claim would be silently exempt from the staleness it declared. An entirely unmarked slot is NOT a control for that case and the row's test asserts so directly: the presence predicate must PASS the plant, or the row is proven by a refusal that is not its own. Both arms carry the valid sibling and both name PERISHABLE, so they differ in the FORM alone. The row carries its prove-rows ARRANGEMENT in the same wave, per the rule lc-231's gap produced, and the mutation is the grammar predicate itself rather than the door's call - folding the call would leave presence firing on other inputs and prove nothing about this one. A MISSING HALF OF THE PARSER, added because this is its first reader: `_resolve_amendments` overwrote the booked value in place, so `slots` held the LAST amendment's text and the ORIGINAL was unrecoverable anywhere. `Item.originals` now keeps it, beside `amendments` and for the reason that field already states. Without it every mark written at BOOKING was invisible on an amended item - and evidence is the most-amended slot at 39%, which is this item's own requirement. A DEFECT FOUND BY EXERCISE AND NOT BY REVIEW, and it is this item's own attack shape turned on its own build: the flag walked the ORIGINAL while the count printed beside it read `slots`, so a flagged claim's report line VANISHED the moment its item was amended for an unrelated reason - the warning stopped being rendered while nothing had been refreshed, which is verbatim the failure astra found in the first design. Both now come from ONE walk (`evidence_events`), with a regression arm over exactly that sequence and a second arm holding the DENOMINATOR after a real re-derivation, since `0 flagged over 0 marks` and `0 flagged over 1 mark` are different facts and only the second says the mechanism ran. PROVEN AT THE EFFECT SITE in a scratch repo, three arms through the real doors: booked past-dated FIRES; an unrelated amendment STILL FIRES; the re-derivation naming the command CLEARS it while the denominator holds. NO-YIELD DISPOSITION, OWNED and sharing W1's pass, writer and event, so it landed on the same line rather than a second one - `lifecycle retire` now names both standing flag lines and says how a later line joins, by being named there, which is the only place the pass looks; without that clause the list is a snapshot that goes quietly stale, the same failure the line exists to prevent one level down. MUST-NOT-BUILD HELD, both halves: nothing grades the re-derivation's RESULT - the check asks only whether the command was NAMED in a later write, because grading whether a re-derivation held would be a checker deciding whether a sentence is true, which is the thing the mark vocabulary exists to avoid; and no freshness rule reads the clock instead of the carrier's order. TWO WRITE-SET ENTRIES NEEDED NO CHANGE, recorded rather than silently skipped: cli.py, because the mark rides the existing `--evidence` and the report rides `item check`, so no new CLI surface was owed; and vocab.py's MEMBER LIST, which updated itself because the registration derives from `items.EVIDENCE_MARKS` rather than restating it - the single-source design working. vocab.py's PROOF PATH did change, and had to: left alone it claimed presence-checking over the one member presence cannot check, which is an assurance wider than its predicate inside the registry built to forbid exactly that. GREEN, each figure from the instrument's own output at b43ed8d: suite 947 OK (+16 here); `lifecycle --test` 125 rows, 125 passed 0 failed 0 could-not-verify 0 raised, CLEAN, the new row reading plant FINDING / control CLEAN; prove-rows 104 of 125 rows carrying an arrangement, 102 PROVEN, the only two non-green the same pre-existing pair booked at lc-106 and lc-197; `kind check` CLEAN at 29 kinds; `item check` CLEAN, conservation 248 CLEAN; absence-scan clean.

## lc-246
grade: DONE
requirement: THE REPO ROSTER LISTS ONE REPO WHILE TEN CARRY A DECLARATION, so every mechanism generated over that roster - lane list's board, and any reading of law 25's EVERY DECLARED REPO that trusts it - is scoped to a population nobody chose and renders the rest as absent rather than as unlisted. lifecycle itself is not on its own roster. Record: docs/2026-09-19-arc-kind-design.md, the reach section's closing wave finding; attack findings B4 and astra a3; LEDGER.md:106 for the ten.
goal: enforce-the-invariants
write-set: ~/.config/lifecycle/repos,plugin/cli/lifecycle_core/lanes.py,plugin/cli/lifecycle_core/refusals.py
done-criterion: THE POPULATION QUESTION IS ANSWERED IN ONE PLACE AND THE ANSWER IS EXECUTABLE. Either the roster file is completed to the declared-repo population and something DETECTS its divergence from that population afterwards, or the roster is redefined as a deliberate SUBSET and the reach-taking verbs stop reading it as the population - one of the two, stated, never both. Done when a divergence between ~/.config/lifecycle/repos and the find-derived declared-repo population is a FINDING with a registered row, red-first on a roster missing a repo that carries a declaration, and green on one that matches; and when the finding's text names which of the two readings the repo took. THE WRITE OUTSIDE THIS REPO IS NOT THIS DESK'S: ~/.config/lifecycle/repos is machine state under the operator's home, so this entry's in-repo half is the detector and the roster edit itself halts to the round desk with the divergence output. MUST-NOT-BUILD: no verb that silently REWRITES the roster from a find sweep - a tool that repairs the population it is supposed to measure cannot then report on it; and no hardcoded repo list anywhere in the package (law 6).
evidence: MEASURED at this desk 2026-09-19, both sides of the divergence: ~/.config/lifecycle/repos carries one repo path, ~/dev/Gunther-Schulz/dotfiles, and lifecycle itself is absent from it; find ~/dev -path '*/.claude/lifecycle.json' returns 19 files, of which 9 are throwaway statiker run arms under ~/dev/local, leaving 10 real declaring repos. MEASURED, an independently-taken second reading agreeing on that coordinate: LEDGER.md:106 already records TEN repos carry a declaration, written by an earlier desk from its own sweep. DERIVED, read at the artifact: CLAUDE.md's router section states lane list is GENERATED over this file and that an absent roster is BROKEN rather than an empty board - which makes an INCOMPLETE roster the case the router has no answer for, since a listed-but-thin roster renders as a clean board. DERIVED: the same file is what makes law 25's EVERY DECLARED REPO ambiguous, which is why the reach act names its population BY COMMAND instead and this entry exists to settle which source is authoritative.
blocked-by: NONE
closed-reason: 2026-09-19 2026-09-19 CLOSED ON THE IN-REPO HALF, which is the whole of this entry's own `Done when` clause; the roster EDIT is named by the entry itself as not this desk's and HALTS to the round desk with the divergence output, delivered in the same message as this close. THE CRITERION'S THREE CONDITIONS, each met: a divergence between the roster and the find-derived declared-repo population IS a FINDING with a REGISTERED row; it is red-first on a roster missing a repo that carries a declaration (`roster_population_diverges`, plant FINDING / control CLEAN, and the control declares the SAME contract and matches, so the arms differ in the divergence alone); and the finding's text NAMES WHICH OF THE TWO READINGS THE REPO TOOK. Today it has taken NEITHER, and that is what the second row says. THE READING IS DECLARED IN THE FILE, not inferred from it, and that is this build's one real design choice. The roster either IS the declared-repo population or is a deliberate SUBSET, and the two are INDISTINGUISHABLE BY CONTENT: a roster listing one repo out of ten is either wrong or intended and nothing in the list can say which — only its author can. So the file carries `# contract: population` or `# contract: subset`, and a roster declaring neither fires `roster_population_undeclared`: until it says, the divergence CANNOT be graded, because the same numbers are the intended state under one reading and a defect under the other. THE TOOL DEFAULTS TO NEITHER — a default here would answer, in the tool's own voice, the one question this entry exists to make someone answer. THE SCOPE IS THE CALLER'S CLAIM: `--under` is repeatable and REQUIRED for a verdict, and with none given the verb answers COULD NOT VERIFY naming what is missing — a sweep with no roots finds nothing, and nothing reads exactly like a roster that already lists everything. A root baked into the package would be both a machine path (law 6) and a reach nobody chose, which is the same defect one level up from the roster it grades. A DEFECT FOUND BY THE ROW'S OWN CONTROL, and the repair is a shared predicate rather than a fixture patch: the roster side resolved each line through `resolve_repo_row`, which refuses a directory that is not a git work tree, while the sweep counted ANY directory holding a declaration. The control listed both scratch repos and still reported them missing. The consequence had it shipped is what prices it: a stray declaration in a non-git directory would report ABSENT FROM THE ROSTER forever and NO edit to the roster could ever clear it — a finding with no repair, which trains the override reflex exactly as a guard firing on legitimate work does. Two populations compared by two predicates are not comparable at all; both sides now resolve through the one resolver. TWO ROWS BECAUSE THERE ARE TWO STATES WITH DIFFERENT REPAIRS — an undeclared contract is repaired by one comment line from the file's author, a declared-population divergence by the missing paths — and each control varies exactly one property: the undeclared pair DIVERGES IDENTICALLY in both arms so only the contract line differs, and the diverges pair declares the SAME contract in both arms so only the match differs. Both carry prove-rows ARRANGEMENTS in the same wave, per the rule lc-231's gap produced, and NEITHER takes `if False:`: the undeclared mutation makes the reader ANSWER `population` where the file declares nothing, which is precisely the defect, and the diverges mutation empties the OMITTED direction alone, since folding the branch would silence the extra-repo direction in the same stroke. One anchor was re-pointed after the tool REFUSED it for cutting mid-line — the arrangement file accepts whole-line runs only, so it answered COULD NOT VERIFY rather than a false PROVEN, which is the staleness half of the anchor rule working. MUST-NOT-BUILD HELD, both halves: there is NO verb that rewrites the roster from the sweep, and the refusal says so in its own text — a tool that repaired the population it is supposed to measure would confirm its own edit on the next run and have no independent reading left to give; and no repo list is hardcoded anywhere in the package. MEASURED LIVE, reproducing this entry's own figures independently: 1 listed, 19 swept under ~/dev, 18 absent from the roster, 0 listed carrying no declaration — lifecycle itself among the absent, as the entry records. Nine of the 19 are throwaway statiker run arms under ~/dev/local, leaving TEN real declaring repos, which agrees with LEDGER.md:106's independently-taken earlier reading on that coordinate. WRITE-SET EXTENDED AND NAMED rather than slipped in: cli.py was not in the booked set, and a detector with no invocation surface is a mechanism with no consumer — the assumed-delivery class this repo books against. `lane population` ships with it. WHAT REMAINS AND WHOSE IT IS: the roster edit itself, at ~/.config/lifecycle/repos, which is machine state under the operator's home. It needs BOTH a contract line and — under the population reading — the 9 or 18 missing paths, and which of those two numbers is right IS the decision this detector exists to force. Halted to the round desk with the divergence output rather than left as a silent completion. GREEN, each figure from the instrument's own output at be30931: suite 964 OK; `lifecycle --test` 127 rows, 127 passed 0 failed 0 could-not-verify 0 raised, CLEAN, both new rows reading plant FINDING / control CLEAN; prove-rows 106 of 127 rows carrying an arrangement, 104 PROVEN, the only two non-green the same pre-existing pair booked at lc-106 and lc-197; `kind check` CLEAN at 29 kinds; `item check` CLEAN, conservation 248 CLEAN; absence-scan clean.
closed-ref: 8b2ab7c, be30931

## lc-254
grade: DONE
requirement: NOTHING PUTS A DUE READ IN FRONT OF A SESSION: the evaluation half is built and no surface consumes it, so a kind whose moment fires is indistinguishable from one that did not. Surface it on the ACT — when a verb runs, name the kinds whose derived moment that act fired — costing no context when no verb runs and adding no always-on channel. Record: the booked design §4 Part B and §5 D2. The surfacing rides main() in cli.py — the ONE dispatch point already observing every act — because a per-verb copy in verbs.py would be a second implementation of a thing the system has one of; the due-for-this-act lookup belongs beside the derivation in declaration.py.
goal: enforce-the-invariants
write-set: plugin/cli/lifecycle_core/cli.py,plugin/cli/lifecycle_core/declaration.py,test/test_declaration.py,test/test_verbs.py
done-criterion: A verb invocation whose act matches a kind's derived moment prints a due-read line naming that kind; one matching none prints nothing. RED-FIRST, two arms from the live repo: today EVERY verb run prints no such line (baseline), and after the change a run of a verb that is a declared reader of a kind prints it while a run over an unrelated kind still prints none. MUST-NOT-MOVE: no always-on output — a session that runs no verb sees nothing added, and the erosion rider licenses no context addition here.
evidence: MEASURED (lifecycle-ef, 2026-09-20): firelog.fire is called once per invocation from main(), and a baseline/act/delta run (1,301,044 lines, three verb runs, +3 lines read back carrying verb/repo/outcome/detail) shows the act is already observed. DERIVED (the booked design §2): lifecycle observes acts only — a reply, a reasoning step and a direct file read are invisible to every mechanism here, so an act-anchored surface is the only one available. RELAYED (lifecycle-ef join): cli.py same-region holder is lc-181 only, of 12 file-grain holders. RELAYED: D2 GO as recommended (operator 2026-09-20, ledgered).

## lc-247
grade: DONE
requirement: THE DIGEST'S `[session-read]` MARKER CLAIMS MORE THAN ITS PREDICATE ESTABLISHES, on the surface a session reads to learn what the repo holds. Its legend says the marker names "a kind no verb reads for you"; the predicate asks only whether ANY reader mentions `session`. A kind read by BOTH a session and a verb therefore renders as though nothing reads it for you, which is the assurance-wider-than-predicate class (the mechanism's own words about itself) in a pointer surface - and a pointer surface is exactly where a reader stops looking. Record: found at the build desk 2026-09-19 while declaring the arc kinds.
goal: enforce-the-invariants
write-set: plugin/cli/lifecycle_core/declaration.py,test/test_declaration.py
done-criterion: The marker's PREDICATE and its LEGEND say the same thing. Either the predicate narrows to what the legend claims - marked only where no reader is a `verb:` reference - or the legend narrows to what the predicate establishes, and the wording changes with it. RED-FIRST: a kind declaring BOTH a session reader and a verb reader must render differently from one declaring a session reader alone; today they render identically, which is the defect. CONTROL: a kind with only a session reader keeps its marker, so the repair is not simply deleting the marker. MUST-NOT-BUILD: nothing that changes what `--digest` is FOR - it stays a pointer surface and never becomes an authority on content (lc-219's own boundary).
evidence: MEASURED at the build desk 2026-09-19, over the live declaration: 12 of the 28 registered kinds declare BOTH a session reader and a `verb:` reader - journal entries, laws, audits, directives, refusal-registry, cli source, investigation records, plugin cache versions, design notes, begehung findings, desk state, and arcs. ELEVEN of those twelve predate this session's arc declaration, so the defect is pre-existing and widespread rather than introduced by it; arcs is merely the specimen that made it visible. MEASURED, the predicate itself, read at the artifact: declaration.py marks `[session-read]` on `any("session" in str(r) for r in reads)`, while the legend printed two lines above it says the marker means a kind no verb reads for you. DERIVED: the two are different questions, and the wider one is the one printed - so eleven kinds carry a marker that is false about them, on the surface lc-219 built for a session to learn the repo's map from.

grade: DONE

Its refusal is a registered roster row admitted on lc-142's PAIR: at its real anchor the run reads rows changed: <the row>, and re-pointed at an inert anchor it reads rows changed: NONE and FAILS with 'the row did NOT change' — both quoted in the closing report. The verb's own output states the proxy bound: read means read-through-the-verb, and a direct file open is not counted. MUST-NOT-MOVE: kind show stays the STAGES surface, this verb never rewrites the body it prints.
evidence: MEASURED at this desk 2026-09-20: kind show laws prints stages (home, writer, reader, staleness, exit, growth, trigger) and no body. MEASURED (lifecycle-ef): lifecycle kind --help lists {list,moments,check,sweep,show}; every invocation already writes one fire line with an optional detail field, so the record half needs no new carrier. DERIVED (the booked design): routing the read through a verb converts an unobservable act into an observed one — the only way the surfaced-vs-read number exists. ## lc-116
grade: DONE
requirement: ledger add is the only carrier-writing verb with no --no-commit escape, so a caller batching several ledger lines cannot own their commit the way it can for every item verb. item add, item amend, item promote and item close all carry the flag (cli.py:309, :333, :347, :371); ledger add gains its commit contract in lc-56 but deliberately ships without the flag, because mirroring it needs a cli.py parser change that lc-56's write boundary excludes. Surfaced by the lc-56 lane's grounding round as a brief contradiction, ruled at the drain desk 2026-09-13: ship the contract, book the flag
goal: enforce-the-invariants
write-set: plugin/cli/lifecycle_core/cli.py,plugin/cli/lifecycle_core/verbs.py,test/test_ledger.py
done-criterion: ledger add --no-commit writes the line and prints the SAME NOT COMMITTED text every other verb prints, from the SAME function — commit_paths' skip branch at verbs.py:531-535, never a second spelling of that sentence. RED-FIRST: against the post-lc-56 build, ledger add --no-commit is rejected at argparse; that red is an ARGPARSE REJECTION and therefore does NOT count as the defect red (it scores identically against a build that accepts the flag and still commits) — the counting red is the flag accepted and the commit happening anyway, so the arm must be written against a build carrying the flag with the skip un-wired. MUST-NOT-MOVE, three-fold: ledger add WITHOUT the flag keeps lc-56's unconditional-commit behaviour exactly; the three other ledger.append call sites (verbs.py:805 supersede, :2120 decision, :2142 dropped) are unchanged; and the four existing --no-commit verbs keep their exact current text and semantics
evidence: VERIFIED AT THE DRAIN DESK 2026-09-13 at 06d8281, opened at the code and not relayed. The flag exists on exactly four verbs: cli.py:309 add, :333 amend, :347 promote, :371 close — grep on the flag string, which is the invariant every such parser line must carry. commit_paths already implements the skip branch generically (verbs.py:531-535, printing NOT COMMITTED and returning CLEAN), so the escape needs no new sentence anywhere, only wiring — this is why the change introduces no concept its site does not already have. DEPENDENTS ESTABLISHED, same session, searched on the function name rather than a chosen idiom: cmd_ledger_add has exactly ONE caller (cli.py:752) so no verb invokes it internally; ledger.append has three other call sites, all of which already reach commit_paths through touched. DISCRIMINATING CONTROL that this is a real gap and not a style preference: item amend --no-commit was exercised THREE TIMES at this desk today to batch carrier amendments into one commit, which is precisely the batching use case ledger add cannot serve. JOIN RUN BEFORE BOOKING: grep over ITEMS.md returns zero hits for no-commit; ITEMS-DONE.md:166 is lc-25, the closed sibling that gave item add this contract — the parent of this entry, not a duplicate of it

MUST-NOT-MOVE, three-fold: ledger add WITHOUT the flag keeps lc-56's unconditional-commit behaviour exactly; the three other ledger.append call sites (verbs.py:805 supersede, :2120 decision, :2142 dropped) are unchanged; and the four existing --no-commit verbs keep their exact current text and semantics WIDENED 2026-09-24: the same flag, from the same commit_paths skip branch, on every arc verb that writes a line (arc premise, belief, narrow, advance, yield, reopen, disposition, deadline, verdict) - arc open and arc close stay single-act commits because each moves a counter with the body.
